| **15.8 Rewards and punishments** | rewards: charters (GRAMMAR's lens); punishments: the landed tribute and `vassal_trade_coercion`, withheld recognition, the war of submission | per act; wear as typed incidents | burden → resentment (landed); the overlord-weakness memory (landed streak) | per seam |

**15.9 Wear.** [REFINED 2026-10-17 (fp-final) — R4-12.] Every instrument's act mints a typed decaying incident on the sworn edge through the memory weave's one writer (`mintMemoryWeaveIncident`, the `rite_imposed` idiom) — never a turning point. Incident types avoid `overreach` and `autonom…` (`ESCALATION_HINT` would class them, R4 H7): `recognition_withheld · appointment_imposed · faction_backed · charter_granted`. The weave is lit nowhere: lighting it is a declared same-seed shift on the six presets where the relationship rules run. [REFINED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — THE PATROLS' request (PR §7.6, OD-PR-26; the SEAMS-r1-2 registration idiom): `riders_quartered`, a season of the root's riders on a member's roads (§15.12), is REGISTERED in this closed list in PL's own commit. The list becomes `recognition_withheld · appointment_imposed · faction_backed · charter_granted · riders_quartered`, each joining the landed frozen `relationshipEvolution.js :: MEMORY_WEAVE_INCIDENT_TYPES` (today `route_seized · rite_imposed · elite_feud · elite_amity`). `riders_quartered` is free on master (0 src · 0 tests). Executed (`cure/D-PL-r2b-probe/regex.out`): it is matched neither by `ESCALATION_HINT` nor by the revanchism wound set, `grievanceRead.js :: WOUND_TYPE_RE` (`/war|betray|tribute|conquest|occupation|sack|raid|seiz|impos|contest_loss|contest_forestalled/i`). Measured beside it: `appointment_imposed` DOES match the wound set (`impos`). That is the landed set's stated intent ("any future imposition/seizure incident"): an imposed appointment is a wound that deepens the member's revanchism toward its liege where the weave and the revanchism read are lit. Stated, not changed.] [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — RULINGS-ROUND4 A.7 · RULINGS-ROUND3 §F.6 ("a crown that calls riders from every member in peace pays in resentment across the chain"): `riders_lent` — a member's riders composed by the crown's net onto another member's roads in a season of peace (§4.7 F.6 (c)) — is REGISTERED in this closed list in PL's own commit, which becomes `recognition_withheld · appointment_imposed · faction_backed · charter_granted · riders_quartered · riders_lent`. Free on master (0 src · 0 tests); executed, it matches none of `ESCALATION_HINT`, `DEESCALATION_HINT` and `WOUND_TYPE_RE` (`cure/D-PL-r4-probe/measure.out`), so a lend is wear, not a wound and not an escalation. In war a lend wears nothing (it is service, §4.7 F.6 (a)).] [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — RULINGS-ROUND5 A.8 ("F.6 (b) prices the sweep as the RESENTMENT PATCH, not `riders_quartered` wear"): `riders_quartered` is RE-HOMED to shape 3 (§4.6) as the precedence friction's ROW carrying its resentment patch (§4.7 F.6 (b)), so it is priced where the weave is dark and never twice where it is lit; the closed list PL registers into `MEMORY_WEAVE_INCIDENT_TYPES` becomes `recognition_withheld · appointment_imposed · faction_backed · charter_granted · riders_lent`; THE PATROLS' cites of it as wear (PR §7.6, §10.3 (h) (b), R21, OD-PR-26) are routed.]

**15.10 The counterforces, each with its landed read.** Burden → resentment (tribute and protection-burden conditions) · the overlord-weakness streak · the break road and its fault line (§14.6) · local legitimacy against appointees · the commons (the domestic rebellion gate) · outside patrons (15.2) · distance (`mappedDistanceWeight`, spatial) · leagues of vassals — the coalition plane's own word, dark while `coalitionLedgerEnabled` is dark (enters the hold as `G`) · the internal party of independence (dark). The overreach cost is not a separate law: it is the wear above, feeding resentment.

**15.11 Pace.** Instruments act within the week of their act and wear over seasons; conversion (§14) runs over years; the hold is their balance and moves only as they move.

**15.12 Patrols considered.** [REFINED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — RULINGS-ROUND2 §E, PATROLS CONSIDERED (THE PATROLS, `docs/DESIGN_FP_ARCH_PR.md`, compiled this round from `briefs/L-PR.md`; items 5, 7, 12, 13, 15 and 18): PL mints nothing for patrols and reads them only through the one reads. Patrol coverage SUBTRACTS from the danger ledger's one presence term and is the raider row's counterforce (item 5), so every danger read PL draws on follows by construction; a patrol's report is a town's first belief of a war's shape (item 13), so a liege's BELIEF of a member's siege or incursion (§4.3) may arrive a week sooner where patrols ride, and a named traveller's road encounter is PR's law (item 15), never PL's. THE CROWN'S RIDERS on members' roads under a hold of `staunch` or firmer (the brief's "firm+", in PL's hold words) are PR's instrument, with no hearing of their own — a §15 control instrument whose counterforce is resentment, priced as the precedence friction's `riders_quartered` ROW and its resentment patch, once a season, where the lord's own riders stayed home (§4.7 F.6 (b) (i)) — a member whose riders went to the levy is COVERED by them instead (§4.7 F.4) [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — A.8: "worn as `riders_quartered` (§15.9)" was wear, lit nowhere]. The root's decision to quarter them reads its own hold, `polityHoldOf(worldState, snapshot, rootId, rootId)` (§3.1 e). A SUB-VASSAL's roads are open to them on that same read: the reach is the one crown-reach read, and with the root's own read at `staunch` or firmer it is never zero — `crownReachAt(worldState, snapshot, rootId, subVassalId, rootId).share01 ≥ POLITY_LIEGE_SHARE.marginal × POLITY_PARAMOUNT_REACH.staunch = 0.02` (OD-PL-44) — so no second condition exists; PR's `staunch` threshold stays PR's. [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r3) — two PL-r2-verify minors: "§3.1 b" (the summons) → §3.1 (e), the quartering's own site; and the sub-vassal line `crownReachAt(…).share01 > 0` is STRUCK — under the same observer's `staunch`+ gate the reach is at least 0.02, so the condition added nothing, and PR's "shuts out a sub-vassal under a frayed link" cannot fire (routed to PR).] The crown's call on a member's OWN riders at war is not PR's hearing: it is this volume's summons, the riders an input line on `call_in_answer` (§4.1, OD-PL-50). A member's patrol on another member's road rides it BY RIGHT OF THE BANNER where the receiving member believes the crown's hold `staunch` or firmer (§3.1 (f)), and otherwise crosses on THE PASSAGE's ladder, where members stand as ALLIES with the ally size tolerance (§4.7 F.3) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — RULINGS-ROUND3 §F.3 · SEAMS-r3-6: "crosses on THE PASSAGE's ladder" at every hold contradicted the banner]; PL §4.5's charter carries tolls only, so no charter carries a patrol (the brief's "by the polity-wide passage charter" is routed to the chair, `cure/D-PL-r2.md` §5). Patrols of FEUDING members clash as the feud itself, and the crown's arbitration (§6) is the remedy. Patrols stay map-only until the host's PR lights; from then the host's patrol facts go OUT through the window's three channels — each dated act (a clash, a catch, a screen, a look's report) in the events window (H9), the week just lived per road half (its presence as it was) as a lens-window row (H23) beside it, and the polity window's patrol inputs per seat (H13) — as the fold's map-side INBOX (h) ↳ and PR14 / OD-PR-36 name them. H16 is the map's INBOUND door, which the host never consumes. [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r3) — the PL-r2-verify minor ("like every other host fact" was false: the fold's (h) ↳ splits the channels, and PL-1 (a) itself sends polity facts per seat through H13): the three channels are named.] No PL wave waits on them.] [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — SEAMS-r2-17: "the CROWN'S ROAD WARDENS" → "the crown's riders" (PR's R-8 struck `warden`: a landed role label, CH's display noun, EC's `route_wardenship`, the map's "wardens' terms"). SEAMS-r2-10: PL no longer co-signs a second summons kind for the same men (§4.1, OD-PL-50). RULINGS-ROUND2b HOST-r2-5 (the chair's error corrected): "opens H16" → the events window. SEAMS-r2-20 (routed to PR): the root's own read and the sub-vassal line are written here for PR to cite.]
